T ;   
While T contains less than n-1 edge and E not empty do 
begin 
choose an edge (v , w) from E of Lowest cost; 
delete (v , w) from E; 
if (v , w) does not create a cycle in T 
then add (v , w) to T 
else discard (v , w) 
end; 
if T contains fewer than n-1 edge then print ("no Spanning tree");
